# Agents · T · part 15

1,060 entries, test-writer to tester. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **test-writer**: Test writing specialist. Use after implementing any new function, endpoint, or feature. Triggers on: "write tests for this", "add test coverage", "test this function", after a bug fix (regression test needed), before shipping any feature. ([abhinav-mittal33/bob-banking-assistant](https://github.com/abhinav-mittal33/bob-banking-assistant/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-writer.md) · 3 copies)
- **test-writer**: Writes tests for a feature based ONLY on its spec or requirements, without reading or running the implementation. Launch in parallel with code-writer and give it ONLY the spec, never code-writer's output. Skip entirely if the project has no test runner — check with scripts/detect-stack.py first. ([abigayleh/claude-workflow-plugin](https://github.com/abigayleh/claude-workflow-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/test-writer.md))
- **test-writer**: Write golden and widget tests for abigotado.dev. Tests mirror lib/src by feature and only exist if a real bug could break them. ([abigotado/abigotado_dev](https://github.com/abigotado/abigotado_dev/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-writer.md))
- **test-writer**: Writes pytest unit and integration tests for the campaignfinance pipeline. Writes under tests/ and app/tests/ only — never modifies app/ source. ([Abstract-Data/campaignfinance-2023](https://github.com/Abstract-Data/campaignfinance-2023/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-writer.md))
- **test-writer**: Writes pytest tests for Python projects. Use when adding tests for new logic, backfilling tests for legacy code, or filling gaps the Reviewer flagged. ([Abstract-Data/RyanData-Address-Utils](https://github.com/Abstract-Data/RyanData-Address-Utils/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-writer.md))
